*Christian Wald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究利用留一预测提升离散数据的连续扩散生成效果；在文本与分子图生成实验中，基于留一预测的方法均优于相应的无约束预测基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续扩散模型可在嵌入空间中通过随机动态生成离散数据。若模型预测某个坐标时能直接利用该坐标自身受扰后的状态，任务可能变得过于容易，从而削弱模型学习坐标间依赖关系的动力。论文因此研究仅依据其他坐标受扰状态进行预测的留一方法。

Method: 研究对象为带跳跃的扩散过程：跳跃会将坐标重置为类别值对应的嵌入。论文证明，在给定假设下，若坐标重置完整、时钟与状态无关，重置目标采用精确的留一概率，且漂移项采用完整的终点后验，则过程能保持预设边缘分布。此外，可通过解析似然补全从留一概率构造该漂移，无须额外调用神经网络。实验比较无约束预测、留一预测及补全后的预测，并通过受控干预考察模型对局部输入和上下文的依赖。

Result: 在文本八、语言建模基准一文本生成及锌类分子图生成的实验中，留一预测相关方法在三个数据集上均改善了关键生成指标，相较于匹配的无约束预测基线表现更好。论文还分析了解析似然补全的作用及模型对局部输入和上下文的依赖。

Conclusion: 研究表明，限制预测器使用目标坐标自身的受扰状态，有望促使模型更多利用坐标间依赖，并改善多类离散数据的生成表现。理论保证依赖于论文所述的过程假设，而实验结论来自文本和分子图任务。

Abstract: Continuous diffusion models generate discrete data through stochastic dynamics in an embedding space. We consider jump diffusions that additionally reset coordinates to embeddings of categorical values. A coordinate's own corrupted state can make prediction easy, potentially reducing the pressure to learn dependencies between coordinates. We study leave-one-out (LOO) prediction, in which each coordinate is predicted from the corrupted states of the other coordinates. We show that, under stated assumptions, jump diffusions with complete coordinate resets and state-independent clocks preserve the prescribed marginals when reset destinations use exact LOO probabilities and the drift uses the full endpoint posterior. We further show that this drift can be constructed from LOO probabilities by analytic likelihood completion without additional network evaluations. We evaluate unrestricted, LOO, and completed predictions on Text8 and LM1B text generation and ZINC250k molecular graph generation. LOO-based variants improve key generation metrics over matched unrestricted controls on all three datasets. Controlled interventions assess reliance on local inputs and context, and we analyze the effect of analytic likelihood completion.

</details>


### [173] [From Benchmarks to the Bench: Preference, Correspondence and Selectivity in Bidirectional Drug-Target Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26369)
*Jiangjie Qiu,Chengrui Xu,Xiaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，药物—靶点相互作用模型的推荐结果彼此差异显著，即使测试表现相近，模型给出的候选名单也可能大不相同；因此评估模型不能只看基准测试，还应考察其能否支持实际实验决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现已知靶点的活性分子，以及为现有药物寻找潜在靶点，是药物发现与再利用的重要任务。模型虽可大规模筛选药物—靶点组合，但实验资源有限，必须优先验证少量候选。因此，模型是否能稳定、有效地推荐值得实验检验的对象，具有实际意义。

Method: 研究比较了13种已发布的药物—靶点相互作用系统，分析对象包括711种美国食品药品监督管理局批准药物的分子结构和614种人类蛋白质。研究考察了模型双向推荐的前十名候选及其重合度，并利用79种分子和65种激酶的已发表抑制活性测量评估命中表现。此外，研究在固定模型架构、训练数据和训练目标的条件下改变随机种子重新训练模型，并比较其筛选推荐。

Result: 对于同一靶点或同一药物，不同模型给出的前十名分子或蛋白质名单完全不重合的比例分别为77.8%和70.1%。部分模型虽能富集活性候选，但将推荐对象换成其他药物或靶点后，命中率变化较小；另一些模型的命中率则明显下降。部分前一类模型在两种药物偏好的激酶不同时，较少能同时正确排出分子和激酶。重新训练的模型即使测试表现相近，在独立筛选目录上的前十名推荐平均也仅约有两个候选重合。

Conclusion: 药物—靶点模型的基准测试表现相近，并不意味着其推荐结果一致或具有同等实验价值。模型评估应超越常规基准指标，进一步考察推荐的稳定性、跨药物和靶点的泛化能力，以及对实际实验筛选和靶点识别决策的支持作用。

Abstract: Identifying active molecules for known targets and potential targets for existing drugs is central to drug discovery and repurposing. Drug-target interaction (DTI) models can rank vast numbers of drug-target pairs, but limited experimental capacity restricts testing to a small number of candidates. We compared the bidirectional recommendations of 13 released systems across 711 molecular structures of FDA-approved drugs and 614 human proteins. For the same target or drug, pairs of models produced completely disjoint Top-10 molecule and protein lists in 77.8% and 70.1% of comparisons, respectively. Even where recommendations overlapped, agreement between some systems largely reflected recurring preferences for the same candidates. Published inhibition measurements for 79 molecules and 65 kinases revealed that some systems enriched active candidates, yet their hit yields changed little when recommendations were matched to different drugs or targets, whereas other systems lost substantially more hits. Some of the former systems also rarely ranked both molecules and kinases correctly when two drugs favoured different kinases. We further retrained the same architecture on the same data, with the training objective fixed and random seeds varied. Despite similar test performance, mean pairwise Top-10 overlap on a separate screening catalogue was about two candidates. Our study moves DTI model evaluation beyond benchmark performance to assess whether model predictions can guide meaningful experimental decisions in drug discovery and target identification.

</details>


### [174] [Evidence from a Multi-Regime Causal Discovery Benchmark: UCMT Algorithms, Comparative Performance, and Controlled Ablation Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26383)
*Mahyar Eslami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究对该因果发现算法族及其组件进行了预注册评估。部分设置下表现优于若干基线，但结果依赖数据集与评价层级，未证明其总体优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现算法的表现会随数据类型和基准环境而变化，因此需要在多种异质基准中系统比较算法，并通过受控消融检验算法内部环节的作用。研究旨在提供预注册的实证证据，同时避免将局部优势夸大为普遍结论。

Method: 研究分为两部分：首先，在多类真实、半合成及高斯网络基准上，将三个持续开发中的算法配置与已有方法进行家族分层比较；其次，对全局配置开展独立预注册的受控组件研究，包括固定骨架下的方向判定消融。研究使用有向精确率与召回率的综合指标进行评估，并报告多重检验校正、运行时间、评价局限及可复现性检查。

Result: 全局配置在部分真实、半合成和高斯网络比较中，按配对运行计算的有向综合指标高于若干基线，但在其他设置中落后于领先的排列搜索方法。基于博斯的第四版配置在特定基准套件上相较博斯基线有明显提升，平均配对差为零点二七九，配对层面的校正后检验显著；但数据集层面的校正结果不显著。固定骨架消融中，全局配置在标准化独立结构因果模型数据上优于改造后的回归排序基线，指标为零点五三七对零点四六八，数据集层面校正后检验显著；其方向判定准确率为零点五六三，且未显示相较其他代表性方法的广泛优势。消融还表明，稀疏性设置和基于信息准则的细化会实质影响部分结果，而仲裁机制和自动机制选择未带来经确认的普遍改进。

Conclusion: 现有证据支持将该方法视为已实现并经过实证研究的算法族，但不支持其整体优于现有方法，也不足以证明独立验证或商业影响。结果解读应考虑不同基准间的表现差异、统计检验层级、多重比较以及运行成本和评价限制。

Abstract: We present an internal, pre-registered evaluation of the UCMT causal discovery algorithm family alongside established alternatives across heterogeneous benchmark regimes, complemented by a separately pre-registered, controlled study of UCMT v2 Global components. The contribution is both empirical and methodological: a family-stratified comparison of three actively developed algorithm configurations and targeted evidence about which internal stages affect their outcomes. In the main benchmark, UCMT v2 Global has higher directed F1 than NOTEARS and DAGMA in selected real, semi-synthetic and Gaussian-network comparisons at the paired-run level, while trailing leading permutation-search methods in other settings. The BOSS-based UCMT v4 produces a CSuite-specific directed-F1 improvement over BOSS (mean paired difference +0.279; pair-level Holm p < 0.001), but the dataset-level adjusted result is not significant (p = 0.188). In a separate fixed-skeleton ablation, UCMT v2 Global exceeds an adapted R^2-SortnRegress orientation baseline on standardized iSCM data (directed F1 0.537 vs 0.468, dataset-level Holm p = 0.031); absolute orientation accuracy is 0.563 and there is no established broad advantage over RESIT, ANM or DirectLiNGAM. Controlled ablations show that sparsity and BIC-based refinement materially affect some outcomes, whereas the examined arbitration and automatic regime-selection mechanisms do not yield confirmed general improvements. We report performance shortcomings, multiple-testing correction, runtime costs, evaluation limitations and reproducibility checks. The results support UCMT as an implemented and empirically investigated algorithm family, not a claim of overall superiority, independent validation or demonstrated commercial impact.

</details>


### [175] [A Complete Geometric Message-Passing Architecture for Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26384)
*Nadav Dym,Tal Amir,Gia Chi Dang,Christopher Morris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种简洁的点云等变消息传递架构，并证明其具有完备表达能力；实验显示它能区分若干基于距离的消息传递网络无法区分的点云。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云架构在计算复杂度和表达能力上差异很大，因此需要判断它们能否区分所有不由刚体变换与点排列联系的输入。已有简单的距离消息传递方法表达能力不足，而已知完备方法往往依赖计算昂贵的高阶机制。

Method: 设计一种针对点云的简洁等变消息传递架构，并从理论上证明其完备性，即能够区分任意两个不受所考虑对称变换联系的输入。研究还将类似的证明思路用于一种经过轻微修改的标量等变图神经网络架构。

Result: 理论上，作者证明所提架构具有完备表达能力；实验上，验证其能够区分一些具有挑战性的点云，而仅依据点间距离进行消息传递的方法无法区分。作者还证明，经过轻微修改的标量等变图神经网络架构同样完备。

Conclusion: 提升点云架构表达能力未必需要高阶等变特征或昂贵的高阶计算。该工作为以较简单的等变消息传递实现完备性提供了理论与实验依据，并为分析其他等变架构的完备性提供了线索。

Abstract: Three-dimensional point clouds are a fundamental data modality, and a large family of machine learning architectures has been developed to respect their natural symmetries to rigid motions and permutations. A principled way to compare these architectures, which vary widely in complexity and cost, is through their expressive power. That is, an architecture is complete if it can distinguish any two inputs not related by the symmetries it respects. Simple distance-based message-passing networks are known to be incomplete, and while there exist complete alternatives, they typically rely on computationally intensive mechanisms like high-order representations of the rotation group or higher-order Weisfeiler-Leman-type tests.
  In this work, we devise a novel, simple, equivariant message-passing architecture for point clouds, and prove that it is complete. We complement our theoretical result with experiments showing that our architecture separates challenging point clouds that distance-based message passing provably cannot distinguish. Our results suggest that high-order equivariant features may not be necessary to enhance the expressivity of point cloud architectures, and can help settle completeness for other equivariant architectures for which such results are currently unknown. We give initial evidence for this by proving completeness of a slightly modified version of SEGNN.

</details>


### [176] [Sequential Anytime Valid Tests for Efficient Inference-Time Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26413)
*Vishesh Kumar Singh,Sayak Ray Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种具备统计错误率保证的自适应停止方法，可在确认某个答案占多数后停止采样，从而降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见的多候选生成与奖励模型筛选方法，对每个提示都使用固定计算预算，造成浪费；已有自适应停止方法依赖启发式阈值，无法严格控制过早停止并选错答案的概率。论文希望在给定错误容忍度后，获得可随时停止且具有统计保证的判断规则。

Method: 先按最终答案对候选进行聚类，并以奖励加权自一致性分数衡量各答案。作者将停止过程建模为序贯检验：基于沃尔德序贯概率比检验的方案在经验上有效，但因领先答案会随采样改变、且检验持续进行，无法提供所需保证。为此，论文提出基于投注超鞅（即证据过程）的奖励加权自一致性方法，为策略产生的每个答案构造检验，使任何时刻错误认证非多数答案的概率均不超过给定容忍度。奖励加权分数影响认证速度，而不影响这一有效性保证。

Result: 实验覆盖多种语言模型、数据集和奖励模型。结果显示，认证所需样本量与多数答案相对优势的倒数信息量成比例，约为对数错误容忍度与相应库尔贝克—莱布勒散度之比；相较基线方法，该方法以显著更低的词元成本取得更高准确率。

Conclusion: 该方法将自适应采样与严格的序贯错误控制结合起来，在保证错误认证概率受控的同时减少计算开销。其效率取决于生成分布中优势答案的显著程度：优势越明显，通常越快完成认证。

Abstract: Inference-time alignment methods, such as Best-of-N (BoN), improve language model outputs by generating multiple candidates per prompt and selecting among them using a reward model. Much of this cost is wasted: a fixed budget is applied to every prompt, although the computation required by prompts varies widely. Adaptive methods halt once sampled answers appear settled, but their thresholds are tuned heuristics with no statistical control over early-stopping errors. We instead seek an anytime-valid stopping rule that takes an error tolerance $α$ as input and halts only once an answer is certified to hold a majority ($θ_a > 1/2$) under the generator's distribution. We aggregate candidates by final answer with a reward-weighted self-consistency (RWSC) score and treat stopping as a sequential test over the resulting clusters. Applying Wald's sequential probability ratio test to these clusters yields SPRT-RWSC, which performs well empirically, but its guarantee fails because the leading answer shifts during sampling, and the run is continuously monitored. Our main contribution, e-RWSC, replaces Wald's test with a betting supermartingale (an e-process) for every answer the policy produces, bounding the probability of ever certifying a non-dominant answer by $α$, uniformly over time and across all answers. The RWSC scores drive the bets, so they affect only how quickly a dominant answer $a^\star$ is certified, not validity; certification takes $O(\log(1/α)/d_{\mathrm{KL}}(θ_{a^\star}\|1/2))$ samples. Experiments across several language models, datasets, and reward models confirm the predicted $1/d_{\mathrm{KL}}$ scaling of cost and show that accuracy improves over baselines at substantially lower token cost.

</details>


### [177] [FEA solver integration of a composite World Model with a Spiking Neural Network memory Block for computationally efficient modelling of defects and variabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26419)
*Bassam El Said,Adam Sobey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多模态脉冲变换器材料代理模型，可追踪复合材料的不可逆内部状态，并用于含缺陷结构的有限元仿真；与实验结果相比，预测误差为百分之零点四至百分之四点八。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纤维增强复合材料的局部结构会因制造工艺和缺陷而变化，因此需要能够实时描述各位置材料响应的模型。并发多尺度方法计算成本过高，而传统代理模型还必须应对任意局部结构、长期状态演化、递归预测稳定性和积分点内存限制等挑战。

Method: 构建复合材料世界模型，由材料结构编码器、用于追踪长期内部状态的脉冲神经网络记忆模块，以及学习短期应变状态的变换器模块组成。训练分为材料结构编码、短时间窗基础训练、递归微调三个阶段；递归微调采用计划采样，以抑制预测误差累积。推理时将模型拆分并嵌入非线性有限元求解器。

Result: 模型针对碳纤维复合材料层合板开展验证，涵盖拉伸、压缩和横向剪切，以及无缺陷和含褶皱情形。与实验结果对比，预测误差为百分之零点四至百分之四点八；脉冲神经网络记忆模块能够有效追踪材料内部状态。

Conclusion: 该方法旨在以紧凑且递归稳定的代理模型替代高成本材料尺度计算，并展示了其用于含嵌入缺陷复合材料结构尺度仿真的潜力。摘要所报告的验证结果具有积极表现，但其适用范围仍需通过更多材料体系、缺陷类型和复杂载荷工况进一步评估。

Abstract: Each fibre-reinforced composite structure has a unique material architecture. The manufacturing methods used and the presence of defects lead to local variations which require material-scale models that can resolve the response in real time at every location in the structure. Concurrent multi-scale schemes provide this capability, but at a prohibitive computational cost for large structures as the number of integration points and time increments scales with size. Surrogates have been studied extensively as replacements for material-scale models to mitigate these computational limitations. A surrogate must represent an arbitrary local architecture including defects, track the irreversible material state over an unknown number of increments, remain stable when driven recursively by its own predictions, and fit within the memory available at every integration point. Here, a Composites World Model based on a Multi-Modal Spiking Transformer architecture is designed to meet these requirements. The proposed architecture combines a material encoder, a Spiking Neural Network memory block that tracks the long-term internal state of the material, and transformer blocks that learn the short-term applied strain state. The system is trained in three stages: material architecture encoding, base training on short time windows, and recursive fine-tuning with scheduled sampling to suppress cumulative error drift. The trained model is disassembled at inference and coupled to a non-linear FE solver, enabling structural scale simulation of composites containing embedded defects. Benchmarks against experimental results for IM7/8552 laminates under tension, compression and transverse shear, for pristine and wrinkled composites, show that the SNN memory block successfully tracks the material internal state and results in prediction errors between 0.4% and 4.8%.

</details>


### [178] [Learning to Defer under Expert Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26440)
*Ziyao Mou,Andrea Wynn,Eric Nalisnick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种能够追踪专家表现变化的学习委派方法，使系统随时间在机器决策与人工专家之间更合理地分配任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习委派方法通常假设专家可靠性固定，但人的表现会因疲劳、认知偏差等因素随时间波动。忽视这种变化可能导致系统将任务交给当下不可靠的专家，降低整体决策质量与安全性。

Method: 提出适应专家漂移的学习委派框架。该方法利用序列模型分析专家近期的决策结果，并将这些历史信息输入委派模块，从而估计专家可靠性的变化，动态调整哪些任务交由人工处理、哪些由机器处理。

Result: 在图像分类、医学诊断和仇恨言论检测三类任务上进行了实验，并考察现实委派条件及不规则专家可靠性变化下的泛化表现。摘要报告称，该方法比既有学习委派方法更能适应多种专家表现变化模式，并改善系统性能与委派决策效果。

Conclusion: 学习委派系统不应将专家视为可靠性恒定的决策者。利用近期表现历史建模其随时间发生的变化，有望提升人工与机器协作的适应性及整体效果。

Abstract: The learning to defer (L2D) framework enables safety in machine learning and AI systems by allocating difficult or critical decisions to a human expert. However, most prior work on L2D assumes fixed expert reliability, overlooking temporal fluctuations in human performance due to factors such as fatigue and cognitive biases. Humans commonly exhibit such substantial, systematic performance shifts across a wide range of domains, from high-stakes settings such as radiology, aviation, and judicial decision-making, to everyday tasks such as crowdsourced image annotation. We propose Expert-Drift-Adapted Learning-to-Defer (EDA-L2D), a novel expert-aware L2D framework that explicitly models these temporal shifts by conditioning the deferral head on recent histories of expert outcomes via a sequence model. This history-aware approach enables adaptive deferral policies that anticipate reliability fluctuations and better allocate decisions between human and machine over time. We provide comprehensive experiments across three diverse tasks -- image classification, medical diagnosis, and hate speech detection -- including generalization analyses under realistic deferral conditions and irregular shifts in expert reliability. Our results show that EDA-L2D adapts more effectively than prior L2D methods to diverse temporal patterns of expert reliability, resulting in improved system performance and more effective deferral decisions. Code is available at https://github.com/ZiyaoMou/L2D-Expert-Drift.

</details>


### [179] [A new algorithmic approach for finding flat minima: critically damped Polyak stepsizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26475)
*Francesco Caporali,Jason M. Altschuler,Boris Hanin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种临界阻尼的波利亚克自适应步长规则，使梯度下降在收敛至插值解的同时偏向较平坦的极小点，并给出相应理论保证与实证支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多自适应梯度方法虽能快速降低损失，却未必倾向于平坦解；已有的平坦性偏置方法也可能无法在有限步长下收敛到插值解。论文旨在设计一种兼顾收敛、损失下降与尖锐度降低的步长规则，并为其提供随时间一致的理论保证。

Method: 作者将经典波利亚克步长乘以四和一个温和的阻尼因子，构造临界阻尼波利亚克步长。乘数四使迭代在极小点流形附近产生首阶等幅振荡，阻尼则推动迭代收敛到该流形。损失足够小时，投影后的迭代在首阶上相当于沿极小点流形，对最大海森矩阵特征值对数进行梯度下降。理论分析适用于极小点流形附近具有局部二次莫尔斯—博特结构的一大类非负损失。

Result: 论文为上述目标类别建立了尖锐度和损失下降的首批随时间一致保证。理论指出，该方法会适应局部曲率，在稳定性边界附近更新；其步长随迭代点到极小点流形的距离缩小，因此能够以有限学习率收敛到局部尖锐度较低的插值解。实验显示，该方法产生的平坦化效果类似尖锐度感知最小化，并在非线性师生网络及预训练大语言模型微调等超出理论范围的任务中降低尖锐度。

Conclusion: 该方法通过临界倍增与阻尼，将局部曲率自适应、插值收敛和平坦解偏置结合起来。理论与实验均表明，它是降低解尖锐度的一种有前景的梯度下降步长策略；不过，现有理论针对特定的局部结构，实验中的更广泛效果尚未由该理论覆盖。

Abstract: We present a new adaptive stepsize rule that biases gradient descent toward flatter minima in both theory and practice, and establish the first uniform-in-time guarantees for both sharpness and loss reduction for a broad class of locally quadratic objectives. Our critically damped Polyak (CDP) stepsizes multiply the classical Polyak rule by $4$ and a mild damping factor. The factor $4$ is critical in that it produces equioscillations around the manifold of minimizers $\mathcal{M}$ to leading order. Damping provably drives convergence to $\mathcal{M}$. CDP thus operates at the edge of stability by adapting its stepsizes to local curvature. Once the loss is small, its projected iterates follow gradient descent on $\log{λ_1(\nabla^2 \mathcal{L})}$ along $\mathcal{M}$ to leading order, paralleling the flatness bias of normalized gradient descent (Arora et al., 2022). Unlike normalized gradient descent, CDP step lengths shrink with the distance to $\mathcal{M}$, enabling convergence to an interpolating solution that locally minimizes sharpness at finite learning rate. We establish our theory for a broad class of non-negative losses with locally quadratic Morse-Bott structure near $\mathcal{M}$, capturing regular interpolation regimes in smooth overparameterized neural networks. Empirically, CDP stepsizes produce a flattening effect similar to that of Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM, Foret et al., 2021), reducing sharpness even in settings beyond our theory, including nonlinear teacher-student networks and fine-tuning of pre-trained LLMs.

</details>


### [180] [Neural Conditional Gaussian Model: Efficient Data Assimilation with Sequential Uncertainty Quantification for Nonlinear Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26463)
*Chuanqi Chen,Nan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经条件高斯模型，在学习到的潜在空间中进行高效数据同化，并支持状态估计、预测与不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分观测的非线性系统需要反复估计隐藏状态及其不确定性，但传统非线性滤波计算开销较大，且通常依赖明确的动力学模型。论文旨在兼顾复杂非线性表达能力与数据同化效率，并让估计结果同时反映状态和不确定性。

Method: 神经条件高斯模型通过编码器将物理状态映射到潜在空间，并在其中学习具有条件高斯结构的动力学，使潜在状态的数据同化更新可以闭式计算。随后，模型利用高斯解码器将潜在后验均值和协方差映射回物理空间，结合局部矩传播与学习到的非线性修正得到物理空间后验统计量。训练时，将这些统计量纳入高斯负对数似然，使均值和不确定性共同影响模型学习。

Result: 在基准非线性动力系统上的数值实验表明，该方法能够准确估计状态并进行预测，同时相较传统非线性数据同化方法显著降低在线同化成本。摘要未提供具体数据集、误差指标或性能提升数值。

Conclusion: 该方法以潜在空间中的条件高斯结构实现高效同化，并通过非线性编码与解码保留物理空间的表达能力。由于模型直接学习动力学，也可在缺少显式控制方程时用于状态预测和模拟，适用于基于部分观测的重复状态估计、不确定性量化与预测。

Abstract: Data assimilation (DA) in partially observed nonlinear systems requires repeated estimation of both hidden states and their uncertainty. Conventional nonlinear filtering can be computationally expensive and often relies on an explicit dynamical model. In this paper, we develop the Neural Conditional Gaussian Model (Neural CGM), a data-driven dynamical model that maps unobserved physical states into a learned latent space, where the dynamics of the latent states follow a conditional Gaussian structure. This structure allows closed-form DA updates in the latent space, while nonlinear encoder-decoder mappings retain the flexibility needed to represent complex dynamics in physical space. A key feature of Neural CGM is its treatment of uncertainty in DA. The latent posterior mean and covariance are mapped to the physical variables through a Gaussian decoder that combines local moment propagation with learned nonlinear corrections. The resulting physical-space posterior statistics depend on the sequentially updated latent distribution rather than on a separate post-processing estimate. The same statistics enter a Gaussian negative log-likelihood during training, so both the posterior mean and uncertainty affect the learned model. Because Neural CGM learns the dynamical model itself, it can also be used for state forecasting and simulation without explicit knowledge of the governing equations. Numerical experiments on benchmark nonlinear dynamical systems show accurate state estimation and forecasting while substantially reducing online assimilation cost relative to conventional nonlinear DA methods. Neural CGM is therefore suited to repeated state estimation, uncertainty quantification, and prediction from partial observations.

</details>


### [181] [Active Feature Acquisition by Distilling an Oracle Teacher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26598)
*Yang Meng,Zixin Ding,Robert L. Grossman,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“预言者模仿器”，通过模仿利用完整训练信息构造的特征价值排序，学习在预算约束下逐实例获取特征的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 获取实例的所有特征可能带来金钱、时间或隐私成本，因此需要在预算有限时选择最有助于预测的特征。现有主动特征获取方法通常优化自行构造的效用目标，而非模仿明确的教师；这也使策略能从教师排序中学到多少难以直接衡量。

Method: 训练阶段利用标签和未观测特征值，构造一个闭式预言者：按候选特征对下游预测器误差的影响进行排序。随后通过软目标模仿，将该排序蒸馏为部署时可用的特征获取策略；部署策略则受限于实际可见信息。

Result: 在多模态医学诊断任务和主动特征获取基准测试集上，该方法在不同预算范围内达到或超过现有方法。摘要报告称，其以不到四分之一的特征获取成本，实现了可达准确率增益的百分之九十。

Conclusion: 将教师显式构造成算法，并以模仿学习训练获取策略，能够取得有竞争力的成本—准确率表现；同时，这种设计使研究者可以直接评估受部署信息限制的策略能够恢复多少教师排序信息。

Abstract: Acquiring every feature of an instance is often costly: a measurement can carry a monetary price, a time delay, or a privacy cost. Active Feature Acquisition (AFA) addresses this by learning a policy that decides, per instance, which features to acquire under a budget. Existing AFA methods train this policy by optimizing a self-constructed utility target rather than distilling an explicit teacher. We construct this teacher explicitly. At training time the label and the unobserved feature values are available, and together they determine the teacher in closed form: an oracle that ranks every candidate by its effect on the downstream predictor's error. OracleImitator distills this ranking into a deployable policy by soft-target imitation. Across multimodal medical diagnosis and the AFABench benchmark, OracleImitator matches or outperforms existing AFA methods across the budget range, recovering 90% of the attainable accuracy gain at under a quarter of the acquisition cost. Because the teacher is an explicit algorithmic object rather than an implicit optimization target, we can also measure directly how much of its ranking is recoverable by a policy restricted to what is visible at deployment.

</details>


### [182] [Grid Cells as Spectral Successor Features: A Reusable Basis for Continual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26504)
*Élodie Côté-Gauthier,Doina Precup,Isabeau Prémont-Schwarz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于参考策略转移矩阵特征向量的持续强化学习方法，使智能体能快速适应目标变化，并在双目标网格世界中保持接近最大回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续强化学习要求智能体在任务变化时既能迅速适应，又能复用对环境的已有知识。后继特征将环境动力学与任务奖励分离，但效果取决于基底特征的选择；一般情况下，后继特征无法由同一状态—动作处的基底特征直接表示，学习它们还可能需要额外网络和经验回放。

Method: 以网格细胞可对应于后继表示特征向量这一发现为设计依据，作者从固定参考策略的转移矩阵中提取特征向量作为基底。对于每个特征向量，其对应的后继特征等于该基底特征乘以已知系数：系数为一除以一减折扣因子与对应特征值的乘积。因此，长期回报可用预测即时奖励的同一组特征表示，无需后继特征网络或经验回放。面对新任务时，只需对奖励进行线性回归；行动则依据参考策略的动作价值贪心选择，相当于对参考策略进行一步策略改进。

Result: 在双目标网格世界实验中，作者采用均匀随机游走作为参考策略，其特征向量对应低频拉普拉斯特征向量。每当目标切换时，该方法的回报仍接近最大值；相比之下，简单后继特征方法和深度神经网络方法在每次切换后都损失了大部分回报。

Conclusion: 恰当选择与环境转移结构相匹配的基底，可以将后继特征及其系数解析化，从而简化持续任务适应并提升目标变化时的稳定性。该方法在单目标切换场景中表现突出，但摘要所述证据主要来自双目标网格世界实验，其在更复杂环境与任务中的适用性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Continual reinforcement learning requires agents to adapt when the task changes while reusing what they know about the environment. Successor features separate dynamics from task-specific reward, but their effectiveness depends on the choice of base features. Taking the identification of entorhinal grid cells with eigenvectors of the successor representation as a design principle, we build this basis from eigenvectors of the transition matrix of a fixed reference policy. With such a basis, the successor features at a state-action pair are a linear combination of the base features at that same pair, which is not true in general, and the coefficients are known in closed form: each successor feature is its base feature multiplied by $θ_i = 1/(1-γλ_i)$, where $λ_i$ is its eigenvalue. The long-term return is thus predicted from the same features as the immediate reward, no successor-feature network or replay buffer is needed, and adapting to a new task reduces to a linear regression on the reward. The agent acts greedily on the action values of the reference policy, which is one step of policy improvement over it and suffices to reach a single goal. In a two-goal gridworld, with the uniform random walk as reference policy, whose eigenvectors are the low-frequency Laplacian eigenvectors, agents built this way maintain near-maximal return across every change of goal, whereas SimpleSF and DQN lose most of their return at each change.

</details>


### [183] [Bio-PM-Guided Synthetic IMU Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26607)
*Deepali Singh,Deepesh Parpani,Aviral Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在预训练生物信号编码器的表示空间中生成惯性测量数据，并证明合成数据能提升跨受试者人体活动识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体活动识别所需的惯性测量数据采集和标注成本较高，而且在一组受试者上训练的模型往往难以推广到未见个体。对于动态特征相似的活动类别，即使数据类别均衡，分类器仍可能表现不佳，因此需要有针对性的数据增强。

Method: 研究不直接合成原始波形，而是用冻结的预训练生物信号编码器将十秒惯性测量窗口转换为嵌入令牌序列，并在该表示空间训练类别条件解码器：一种自回归变换器逐步预测令牌，另一种扩散解码器通过去噪目标学习令牌分布。研究使用分布指标和嵌入空间可视化评估生成质量，以留一受试者验证评估下游分类效果，并提出近似重建原始波形的分块解码器。此外，还将合成令牌增强与三百二十维、考虑重力信息的特征表示结合。

Result: 扩散解码器在“合成数据训练、真实数据测试”设置下取得零点六八的宏平均调和均值，表明生成令牌具有一定真实性并能迁移至真实数据。在五十一名受试者的数据基准上，合成令牌增强与重力感知特征结合后，留一受试者验证的宏平均调和均值由仅使用真实数据时的零点七二九提高至零点八零九；提升具有统计显著性，且主要集中在最具挑战性的活动类别。

Conclusion: 在预训练编码器的表示空间中生成高保真合成数据是可行的，并能实际改善跨受试者活动识别。该方法尤其有望缓解难分类活动类别的数据不足问题，但摘要未提供原始波形重建质量或各解码器的详细对比结果。

Abstract: Collecting and labeling inertial measurement unit (IMU) data for human activity recognition (HAR) is expensive, and models trained on one group of subjects often fail to generalize to unseen individuals. Even with balanced data, classifiers struggle to distinguish dynamically similar activity classes and benefit from targeted augmentation. We explore whether synthetic IMU data generated in the representation space of a pretrained biosignal encoder, Bio-PM \cite{b1}, can serve as a practical augmentation source. Rather than synthesizing raw signals, we leverage Bio-PM's frozen encoder to convert 10-second IMU windows into token embedding sequences and train class-conditional decoders in this space: (1) an autoregressive transformer decoder that predicts tokens sequentially, and (2) a diffusion decoder that learns the token distribution through a denoising objective. We analyze generation realism through distributional metrics and embedding-space visualizations, evaluate downstream utility through leave-one-subject-out HAR classification, and present a PatchDecoder for approximate raw waveform reconstruction. Our diffusion decoder achieves a Train-on-Synthetic-Test-on-Real (TSTR) macro-F1 of 0.68, demonstrating that generated tokens are realistic enough to train a classifier that transfers to real data. Combining synthetic-token augmentation with a 320-dimensional gravity-aware feature representation raises LOSO macro-F1 from 0.729 to 0.809 over a real-only baseline across 51 subjects on the WISDM benchmark, with statistically significant gains concentrated in the most challenging activity classes. These results demonstrate that high-fidelity generation in a pretrained encoder's representation space is achievable and practically useful for cross-subject HAR augmentation.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [LSP-RAG: Latent Scoring Perspectives for Knowledge Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25229)
*Haochen Liu,Guanghui Min,Song Wang,Yada Zhu,Chen Chen,Jundong Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向知识图谱检索增强生成的新框架，通过多视角提示评分提升轻量语言模型在多跳问答中的检索质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽擅长开放域问答，但训练语料不完整会导致事实错误和多跳推理困难。现有知识图谱检索增强方法要么依赖大型模型引导检索、计算成本高，要么使用轻量模型时检索效果不足，尤其难以处理多跳问题。

Method: 该方法为候选知识图谱三元组设计多种潜在评分视角。每种视角结合视角专属软提示与上下文软提示：前者形成不同的条件化评分函数，后者随推理步骤更新，编码问题及此前检索到的三元组，以维持多跳检索的相关性和一致性。系统自适应聚合各视角的评分信号，而非预先指定语义角色。

Result: 论文报告称，该方法在多个基于知识图谱的问答基准上优于现有检索系统，包括依赖规模更大的语言模型的系统；同时能在计算资源有限的条件下实现准确、稳健的检索。

Conclusion: 通过结合多视角潜在评分与逐跳更新的上下文提示，该框架改善了轻量语言模型的知识图谱检索能力，尤其适用于多跳问答，并为降低检索增强生成的计算成本提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in open-domain question answering, but often struggle with factual accuracy and multi-hop reasoning due to the incompleteness of the training corpora. A promising solution is Knowledge Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (KG-RAG), which supplements LLMs with structured knowledge retrieved from external knowledge graphs (KGs). However, existing KG-RAG methods either rely on large-scale language models (e.g., ChatGPT) to guide the retrieval process, which leads to high computational costs, or suffer from limited retrieval quality when using lightweight language models, particularly under multi-hop scenarios. We propose LSP-RAG (Latent Scoring Perspectives for Retrieval-Augmented Generation over Knowledge Graphs), a novel KG-RAG framework that enhances hop-wise KG knowledge retrieval through multi-perspective prompted scoring. Each latent scoring perspective is guided by a combination of two types of soft prompts: a perspective-specific soft prompt induces a distinct prompt-conditioned scoring function over candidate triplets, while a contextual soft prompt evolves with each reasoning hop by encoding the query and previously explored KG triplets, enabling the model to preserve consistency and relevance across multi-hop retrieval. Rather than assuming manually defined semantic roles, these latent perspectives are adaptively aggregated to capture heterogeneous relevance signals during retrieval. This design allows LSP-RAG to perform accurate and robust retrieval using lightweight language models. LSP-RAG achieves superior performance across multiple KG-based Question Answering benchmarks compared to existing retrieval systems, including those that rely on much larger language models, demonstrating its effectiveness under limited computational budgets.

</details>


### [185] [Retrieving Patristic References and Modelling Topics in Bullinger's Reformation Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25247)
*Lena Pickartz,Philipp Schaer,Phillip B. Ströbel*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文首次探索在海因里希·布林格书信中自动检索教父文献引用，并以主题建模分析信件议题。词汇检索最擅长识别明确引用；融合词汇与向量检索可改善隐含引用的发现。主题模型的定量提升有限，主题权重也不足以辅助检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布林格的书信记录了宗教改革时期的政治与宗教辩论，其中广泛援引教父著作。然而，计算方法识别这些引用、尤其是未明确标注的隐含引用，仍鲜少受到研究。研究也希望从书信中提取细粒度议题，以辅助理解其思想与历史语境。

Method: 研究比较词汇检索、双编码器向量检索及二者融合，在明确引用和隐含引用两类任务上评估效果，并测试针对拉丁文文本及圣经互文训练的模型能否迁移至布林格书信。另以带种子引导损失和编者摘要向量的主题模型分析议题，并考察主题与历史事件的关系；同时试用大语言模型驱动的概念归纳，探索更易解释的替代方案。

Result: 在向量检索模型中，针对圣经互文微调的拉丁文编码器表现最佳：明确引用的前二十项召回率为百分之八十二，隐含引用为百分之十六。词汇检索在明确引用上更优，前二十项召回率达百分之九十四。由于两类检索找到的隐含引用集合互有差异，融合排序将其前二十项召回率提升至百分之二十二；对相同候选项进行交叉编码器重排可达百分之二十六，但计算成本更高。主题模型相较基线的连贯性提升很小；部分主题在定性及时间分析中对应到已知历史事件，但主题权重仅略高于均匀分布，无法有效支持检索。

Conclusion: 研究建立了在布林格书信中检索教父引用及分析细粒度主题的初步方法。明确引用更适合采用词汇检索，隐含引用则可通过融合词汇与向量检索，以较低推理成本获得实用提升。当前主题模型尚不足以为检索提供有效信息，而基于概念归纳的方案可能是更具可解释性的后续方向。

Abstract: The correspondence of Heinrich Bullinger (1504-1575), Swiss Reformer and Antistes of Zurich, documents the political and religious debates of the Reformation, including extensive appeals to the Church Fathers as authoritative sources. Detecting such references computationally remains largely unexplored. We investigate dense and sparse retrieval for patristic reference detection, and metadata-informed topic modelling as a proxy for the topics and ideas discussed. Prior work on Latin intertextuality has fine-tuned dense retrievers for their respective target domain, and we test how well these models transfer to Bullinger's correspondence. We compare lexical (BM25), dense (bi-encoders), and hybrid methods on explicit and implicit references. A Latin encoder fine-tuned on biblical reuse performs best among the bi-encoders (82%, Recall@20 on explicit and 16% on implicit references), while BM25 outperforms dense approaches on explicit citations (94%, R@20). Because BM25 and dense retrieval recover disjoint sets of implicit references, fusing their rankings raises implicit R@20 to 22%, close to cross-encoder reranking of the same candidates (26%) but without its inference cost. For topic modelling, we extend FASTopic with a seed-guided loss function and editorial abstract embeddings. Measurable coherence gains over the baseline remain minimal, but qualitative and temporal analysis shows that some topics track documented historical events. However, dominant topic weights barely exceed a uniform distribution, making topics unusable to inform retrieval. A pilot application of LLM-based concept induction (LLooM) further points towards a more human-interpretable alternative. Together, these results present a first approach to retrieving patristic references and modelling fine-grained topics in Bullinger's correspondence.

</details>


### [186] [LQ-Rec: Late Quantization for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25256)
*Satoshi Koide,Yueqi Wang,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向序列推荐的非自回归生成式检索方法，将离散化限制在物品侧，并通过理论分析与实验表明，语义标识长度增加时排序质量可持续提升，同时支持灵活压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式检索通常把物品编码为语义标识，再由自回归解码器生成下一物品的编码。虽然这种方式有利于冷启动和存储，但加长标识会增加解码开销与二次方级内存需求，也可能损害排序效果；以往方法还缺少对量化误差如何影响排序的结构性保证。

Method: 提出延迟量化框架：编码器和打分规则均在连续空间中运行，只在输出端对物品进行离散化。物品通过端到端训练的残差量化器生成语义标识，并以标识进行存储和匹配。论文进一步给出加性误差分解和成对排序定理，用于界定量化导致的排序性能下降。

Result: 在四个公开基准上的实验显示，该方法在语义标识变长时排序质量单调提升，并优于已有基线；同一个已训练模型可在无需重新训练的情况下调整压缩程度，在排序质量与推理成本之间进行权衡；此外，在区分记忆与泛化的评测中也表现出更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过将离散化从整个检索流程移至物品输出端，该方法缓解了自回归生成式检索在标识长度上的结构性扩展瓶颈，并为量化误差造成的排序损失提供理论界限。实验结果表明，这一设计兼顾排序效果、压缩灵活性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Generative retrieval (GR) for sequential recommendation compresses each item into a short semantic ID and trains an autoregressive decoder to generate next-item codes from a user history. While appealing for cold-start handling and storage, au- toregressive GR suffers a structural scaling limit: typically, longer semantic IDs reduce quantization error in principle but degrade ranking quality and quadratic memory usage against semantic ID length. We propose Late Quantization (LQ), a non-autoregressive paradigm that confines discretization to the output (item) side of the pipeline. The encoder and scoring rule operate in continuous space, while items are stored and matched as semantic IDs produced by an end-to-end-trained residual quantizer. This architectural shift permits an additive error decomposition and a pairwise ordering theorem that jointly bound the quantization- induced ranking drop, a structural guarantee absent in prior semantic-ID recommenders. Across four public benchmarks, the resulting recommender, LQ-Rec, delivers three capabilities simultaneously: ranking quality improves monotonically with the semantic-ID length and outperforms existing baselines; a single trained model supports anytime compression, tuning ranking quality against inference cost without retraining; and LQ-Rec shows superior generalization on a memorization/generalization partition.

</details>


### [187] [Learning Generative Ranking Policies for Whole-Page Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25527)
*Chen Qiu,Zhigang Hua,Qi Xu,Jizhe Zhang,Sha Hua,Ren Mao,Luke Simon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出整页策略改进方法，联合选择并排序推荐页面中的项目；在多个公开数据集、工业日志数据及合成基准上优于所比较的生成式重排序方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐页面应作为整体优化：页面项目会争夺注意力，也可能相互补充，因此逐项挑选各自最优项目并不能保证整页效果最好。现有生成式重排序方法存在两类不足：把同一个页面级训练信号分配给页面中的所有选择，或模仿训练前构造且随生成器改进而基本不变的目标页面。

Method: 提出整页策略改进，以一个局部页面级目标反复推动当前生成器转向得分更高的页面。该目标可精确拆分为每个位置各自的训练目标：在相同的部分页面条件下，比较不同候选项目所能形成的完整页面得分，使每个位置学习选择对整页更有利的项目，而非仅在单项层面表现更好的项目。

Result: 在涵盖多目标项目选择和受约束排序的两个公开数据集，以及一个工业页面日志数据集上，该方法优于所评估的生成式重排序基线。在具有已知最优页面的合成基准上，该方法得到的页面也最接近最优解。

Conclusion: 结果表明，依据候选项目所引导形成的完整页面来训练各位置决策，有助于改善整页推荐质量；合成基准进一步支持其收益来自更好的页面整体决策。

Abstract: A recommendation page is consumed as a whole: the items on it compete for attention and complement one another, so the best page is not simply the individually best items placed side by side. We study whole-page optimization, which selects and orders the items on a page jointly to maximize the score of the page. The number of possible pages grows combinatorially, so good pages must be generated rather than enumerated. Some generative re-ranking methods use the same page-level training signal for every choice in a sampled page. Others imitate target pages built before training, which remain largely fixed as the generator improves. We address both limitations with one local page-level objective, which repeatedly moves the current generator toward pages with higher score. This objective splits exactly into one training target per position, and each target compares candidates at the same partial page by the scores of the complete pages they lead to. Every position therefore learns to choose the item that is better for the whole page, rather than the item that looks best on its own. We call this procedure whole-page policy improvement. On two public datasets, KuaiRand-1K and RL4RS-SeqSlate, which cover multi-objective item selection and constrained ordering, and on an industrial dataset of logged pages, our method outperforms the evaluated generative re-ranking baselines. On a synthetic benchmark with a known optimal page, it also comes closest to the optimum, showing that its improvements come from better whole-page decisions.

</details>


### [188] [Generative News Recommendation Reproduces Popularity: A Memorization-Based Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25606)
*Shotaro Ishihara*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究发现，新闻生成式推荐模型会更频繁地复现训练数据中反复出现的热门标题，导致推荐结果集中度升高、信息多样性下降；去重虽能缓解这一现象，但会损害训练规模与推荐准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐能够直接生成新闻标题，但可能反复推送热门文章，缩窄用户接触的信息范围。论文关注训练数据记忆是否会促成这种信息集中：浏览日志中的热门标题因频繁出现而更容易被模型记住并再次生成。

Method: 研究使用日本新闻服务约两千万条浏览会话，持续训练经过指令微调的八十亿参数大语言模型，使其根据会话内容生成下一篇文章的标题。研究比较生成标题与浏览日志中的标题分布，并考察标题重复频率、逐字及近似记忆程度、提示范围之间的关系；另设置目标标题去重条件进行探索性分析。

Result: 模型生成的标题分布比浏览日志更集中、多样性更低。作为目标反复出现的标题表现出更强的逐字和近似记忆；记忆程度越强的标题，也越容易在更多不同提示下被生成。对目标标题去重后，记忆现象和输出集中度均有所下降，但训练集规模与推荐准确率也显著降低。

Conclusion: 新闻生成式推荐中的热门偏差可能不仅源于用户偏好或排序目标，也可能来自模型对高频文本的优先记忆与复现。缓解这种偏差存在代价：简单去重虽能改善多样性，却可能损害数据利用和推荐效果，因此需要进一步探索兼顾准确率与信息多样性的训练方法。

Abstract: Text-based generative recommenders can go beyond ranking existing items by directly generating item titles or other textual representations. In the news domain, this raises a societal concern that models may repeatedly surface already popular articles and thereby narrow the diversity of information to which users are exposed. We investigate training-data memorization as one mechanism behind this concentration. Using approximately 20 million browsing sessions from a Japanese news service, we continually trained an instruction-tuned Llama 3 8B model to generate the next article title in a session. The generated-title distribution was more concentrated and less diverse than the distribution of titles observed in the browsing logs. Frequently repeated target titles exhibited greater verbatim and approximate memorization, and more strongly memorized titles were generated in response to a wider range of prompts. These findings support a pathway in which popularity in behavioral logs becomes repeated training text, is preferentially memorized, and is subsequently reproduced by a generative recommender. An exploratory target-level deduplication condition reduced both memorization and output concentration, while substantially reducing the training-set size and recommendation accuracy. Our results suggest that popularity bias in generative recommendation may arise not only from user preferences or ranking objectives, but also from the model's tendency to reproduce frequently encountered strings, with important implications for information diversity on news platforms.

</details>


### [189] [DNA: Unlocking Depth Scaling in CTR Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25659)
*Caiyuan Li,Wenjie Wu,Yuheng Huang,Xiao Zhou,Silu Zhou,Ke Chen,Zinan Zhao,Xinrui Zeng,Hanmo Xu,Chaoqun Song,Hua Zong,Zongyuan Wu,Hongbin Zhou,Huimin Yi,Zhixuan Zhang,Dan Ou,Haihong Tang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出双流枢纽注意力，使用户行为序列能够随网络深度按当前请求动态更新，在保持序列长度线性计算复杂度的同时提升点击率预测效果；该方法已部署于淘宝搜索，并带来线上业务增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长用户行为序列是点击率预测的重要信号，交叉注意力能以线性计算复杂度利用这些序列。但传统结构每层都读取同一组行为序列，增加层数只是重复读取固定信息，因此性能提升很快趋缓；淘宝搜索中，层数从十六层增至二十八层，曲线下面积仅提高零点零二个百分点。论文据此认为，限制性能的关键在于行为序列这一键值侧未随深度演化。

Method: 提出双流枢纽注意力。每层先由请求表示（搜索查询、用户特征和场景信息）读取行为序列，再让每个行为标记读取更新后的请求表示，并据此更新自身。更新后的序列供下一层读取，使序列逐层融入当前请求的信息。该更新针对每个请求执行一次，候选项目共享更新后的序列；读取与更新的计算复杂度均随序列长度线性增长。

Result: 在前向浮点运算量相近的条件下，该方法相较交叉注意力堆叠结构，曲线下面积提升零点零八至零点二六个百分点，并且在传统结构性能趋于饱和的深度区间仍持续改善。其四层版本已部署于淘宝搜索；与生产模型相比，线上测试中点击率提升百分之零点八，商品交易总额提升百分之零点五。

Conclusion: 让行为序列随网络层数和当前请求动态演化，可缓解传统交叉注意力反复读取固定序列造成的性能瓶颈。该方法在维持线性复杂度的同时改善离线预测指标，并取得线上业务收益，显示出在工业搜索排序中的实用价值。

Abstract: Long user behavior sequences are among the strongest signals for click-through rate (CTR) prediction. Cross-attention makes these sequences practical for industrial ranking by keeping compute linear in sequence length. Yet the gains from stacking cross-attention layers taper off: on Taobao search, growing a cross-attention stack from 16 to 28 layers adds only 0.02 percentage points (pt) of AUC. We argue that the key-value side is the bottleneck. Each layer projects the same behavior sequence into keys and values, so adding depth gives the model more reads of a fixed sequence. We propose DNA (Dual-stream Nexus Attention), which lets the sequence itself evolve with depth. At each layer, the request representation (the search query, user features and scene) first reads the sequence. Each behavior token then attends to the updated request representation and uses what it reads to update itself, so the next layer reads a sequence shaped by the current request. The update runs once per request, all candidates share the resulting sequence, and both the reads and the update keep linear complexity in sequence length. At comparable forward FLOPs, DNA improves AUC over cross-attention stacks by 0.08 to 0.26 pt and keeps improving in the depth range where those stacks level off. DNA-4 is deployed in Taobao search. In an online A/B test against the production model, it improves CTR by 0.8% and GMV by 0.5%.

</details>


### [190] [Exploring Semantic Table Search in Data Lakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25691)
*Martin Pekár Christensen,Matteo Lissandrini,Katja Hose*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出融合关键词与语义相关性的表格搜索方法，并发现分层可导航小世界索引能显著加速语义搜索，同时保持较高的结果质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据湖中相关数据集的发现仍具挑战。并集和连接搜索适合扩充已有表格，而探索式搜索通常依靠关键词检索少量高相关表格；基于示例元组中命名实体的语义搜索则能发现不同类型的表格。单一搜索信号难以兼顾精确关键词匹配与语义关联，因此需要融合多种相关性信号，并提升语义搜索的可扩展性。

Method: 提出一种融合关键词搜索与语义表格搜索的方案，结合精确关键词匹配和实体语义信号来发现相关表格。论文比较多种结果排序融合方法，包括无监督方法、有监督方法及优劣解距离法；同时研究一种用于表格搜索的学习排序方案。此外，针对语义搜索的预筛选，比较局部敏感哈希索引与分层可导航小世界索引。

Result: 实验显示，许多标准排序融合方法存在局限，而学习方法展现出一定潜力。在语义搜索效率方面，分层可导航小世界索引平均将运行时间提升至原来的约四点七倍效率，同时保留高质量的表格结果。

Conclusion: 融合关键词与语义信息有助于更全面地发现数据湖中的相关表格；学习排序是值得进一步探索的方向。采用更高效的语义预筛选索引，能够在维持结果质量的同时显著加快搜索，为构建有效且高效的语义数据湖发现技术提供了依据。

Abstract: Discovery of relevant datasets within data lakes remains a key challenge. Union and join search are effective techniques for discovering data when augmenting an existing table. In exploratory search, instead, keyword search is commonly adopted to find a relatively small set of highly relevant tables matching some search terms. In this context, semantic search is a new technique that exploits the presence of named entities in exemplar tuples used as queries and in target tables. Hence, keyword search and entity search are able to find a diverse set of tables. In this work, we propose \texttt{Thetis++}, which combines keyword and semantic table search to discover relevant tables that combine the semantic signal with the presence of exact keyword matches. Incorporating multiple paradigms and sources of information allow us to combine the best of multiple worlds. Therefore, we study many alternative techniques for combining rankings and evaluate these for the table search task in data lakes. We explore both unsupervised and supervised techniques, including TOPSIS. Finally, alternative to Thetis++ combining keyword and semantic relevance signals, we study a variant of learning-to-rank for the table search task in data lakes. Our evaluation highlights both the limitations of many standard approaches along with some promising directions offered by learned approaches. Furthermore, while indexes for keyword search are quite efficient, here we provide insights into how to improve the scalability of prefiltering techniques for semantic search, comparing LSH indexes with Hierarchical Navigable Small Worlds. Our experiments show that HNSW drastically improves the runtime of semantic table search by 4.7X on average whilst retaining the high-quality tables. Together, our findings plot the line for effective and efficient data discovery techniques for semantic data lakes.

</details>


### [191] [Can LLMs Resolve Entity Conflicts in Domain Specific NLU Pipelines?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25733)
*Akansha Bhardwaj,Mingbo Cui,Victor Guimaraes,Dominik Kohler,Diana Nicoleta Popa,Meghana M. Reddy,Paolo Rosso*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文比较规则法、多示例提示和低秩适配微调三种实体冲突消解方案，发现微调模型更擅长处理复杂歧义情形，结合提示灵活性与微调能力可能效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于流水线的自然语言理解系统中，多个抽取器可能产生重叠或相互矛盾的结果，实体冲突消解因此至关重要。传统规则方法灵活性不足且难以扩展。论文探讨大语言模型能否利用其广泛知识，改进特定领域自然语言理解流水线中的冲突消解。

Method: 研究比较三种方法：传统规则式冲突消解、多示例提示，以及对拉玛三点一模型进行低秩适配微调。实验包括两种评估设置：一是考察模型复现现有自然语言理解流水线输出的能力；二是在更受控的条件下，评估模型对人工标注数据的判断准确率。

Result: 多示例提示易于适配，也能快速改变模型行为；低秩适配微调模型在复杂且具有歧义的案例上表现更好。总体而言，两种大语言模型方案各有优势，单一方法难以兼顾灵活性与复杂案例表现。

Conclusion: 实体冲突消解可以受益于大语言模型。较有前景的方案是结合多示例提示的适应性与低秩适配微调带来的整体准确率提升，从而构建更全面的冲突消解能力。

Abstract: Resolving entity conflicts is a critical step in Pipeline-based NLU architectures, especially when multiple extractors provide overlapping or conflicting outputs. Traditional rule-based entity conflict resolution methods often lack flexibility and are unscalable. In this work, we aim to study if the broad knowledge of LLMs can be leveraged to resolve entity conflicts in domain specific NLU pipelines. We present an experimental comparison between a traditional rule-based conflict resolution method, and two LLM-based approaches: a multi-shot prompting method using the Llama3.1-8B-Instruct model and a LoRA fine-tuned Llama3.1-8B-Instruct model. The evaluation was conducted under two setups: (1) a broad assessment of LLM's capability to match existing NLU pipeline output, and (2) a more controlled scenario assessing the LLM accuracy on human-labeled data. Results show that while multi-shot prompting offers ease of adaptation and rapid behavioral changes, the fine-tuned model delivers superior performance on complex, ambiguous cases. Ultimately, the best approach appears to be a combination of both strategies, leveraging multi-shot prompting's flexibility alongside the broad accuracy gains of LoRA fine-tuning for a more comprehensive performance.

</details>


### [192] [CACO: Coverage-Aware Coordinated Optimization for Continuous Generative Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25738)
*Pan Zhang,Nan Zhuang,Yongkang Zhang,Kun Xú,Zhongtao Jiang,Zhiwei Wang,Kun Xǔ,Youhuan Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出覆盖感知协同优化方法，在不改变基础生成检索器、几乎不增加推理延迟的前提下，使多个查询覆盖更互补的物品，从而提升召回率并减少查询间重复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续生成式检索器通常优化查询分布的多样性或目标兴趣的区分度，却忽略多个查询对应的近似最近邻检索结果之间的集合几何关系。因此，查询可能集中到重叠的物品邻域，降低固定检索预算下的整体覆盖率。此外，离散的前若干名检索过程不易直接用于端到端优化，单目标监督也难以优化集合覆盖。

Method: 提出轻量级自监督后训练框架覆盖感知协同优化，适用于冻结的流匹配和扩散式检索器。方法利用参考查询样本的经验检索频率作为相关性代理，构造无需标签的覆盖目标；通过协同查询调整网络，结合查询间双向注意力和门控相对方向，并以有界切空间投影与球面回缩更新查询。训练时使用切向反对称扰动，并通过反事实单查询替换估计每个查询的边际收益，从而在不可微的近似最近邻检索下获得平滑集合覆盖目标的无偏梯度估计。

Result: 在离线贪心选择实验中，该覆盖目标在全部三十种基础模型与数据集组合上提升召回率。在八个公开基准和一个包含二十四个分区的工业流式数据集上，相比最强基线，百名召回率最高提升百分之二十三点七，四百名召回率最高提升百分之二十三点九；查询间重叠减少百分之五十三点三至百分之六十四点九，推理延迟仅增加百分之零点五至百分之三点零。

Conclusion: 研究表明，生成式检索的多查询优化应直接关注检索结果集合的互补覆盖，而不仅是查询本身的多样性。该方法无需标签、可在冻结检索器上后训练，并以较小延迟开销显著改善覆盖与召回；摘要报告的证据涵盖多种模型、公开基准及工业数据场景。

Abstract: Continuous generative retrievers powered by flow matching and diffusion sample multiple queries in item embedding space for parallel approximate nearest-neighbor (ANN) search under fixed retrieval budgets. However, optimizing distribution diversity or target-level interest separation ignores set-level retrieval geometry: sampled queries collapse onto overlapping ANN neighborhoods, while discrete top-K lookup and single-target supervision block end-to-end coverage optimization. We introduce CACO (Coverage-Aware Coordinated Optimization), a lightweight self-supervised post-training framework that coordinates queries from frozen flow-matching and diffusion retrievers toward complementary item coverage with negligible additional inference latency. CACO uses empirical retrieval frequencies across sampled reference queries as a relevance proxy, formulating a label-free coverage objective that improves recall across all 30 backbone-dataset settings in offline greedy selection experiments. To avoid the inference overhead of generating a large query pool, CACO employs a coordinated query adjustment network that combines inter-query bidirectional attention with gated relative directions, finally updating queries by bounded tangent-space projection and spherical retraction. During training, CACO applies tangential antithetic perturbations and measures each query's marginal gain via counterfactual single-query replacement, yielding an unbiased gradient estimator of smoothed set coverage across non-differentiable ANN search. On eight public benchmarks and an in-house 24-partition streaming industrial dataset, CACO improves Recall@100 by up to 23.7% and Recall@400 by up to 23.9% over the strongest baseline, cuts inter-query overlap by 53.3%-64.9%, and adds only 0.5%-3.0% inference latency.

</details>


### [193] [LINEAGE: A Benchmark for Empirical Lineage Retrieval in AI Scientific Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25845)
*Pangjing Wu,Xu Yuan,Wenqi Fan,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出人工智能科学文献中的经验比较谱系检索基准，评估系统能否找出被后续论文明确用作实验比较基线的先前方法及其关联论文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学文献检索基准多以主题相关性或引文关系作为监督信号，难以识别研究中实际用于实验比较的先前方法。随着研究智能体承担文献综合与实验规划任务，准确检索这类经验比较关系变得越来越重要。

Method: 作者定义经验谱系关系：若一篇论文明确将某先前论文的方法用作实验比较基线，两者即构成关联，并据此建立谱系图。进一步提出谱系构建器，将异构论文记录与全文中的基线证据转化为经过校准的谱系图；图中多跳邻域作为检索真值，用于结构化评估。基准覆盖2018至2025年间17个顶级人工智能会议与期刊，共收录121,048篇论文、67,502个图节点、164,948条谱系边、3,950个图连通分量，以及80,000条多维检索查询。

Result: 对19种有代表性的检索系统进行实验后发现，经验谱系检索仍具挑战性：检索表现会随谱系距离增加而显著下降。结构指标还显示，单独命中的论文往往不足以恢复彼此紧密关联的经验比较邻域。

Conclusion: 该基准将文献检索评估从主题匹配和引文关联扩展到实验比较谱系，为研究智能体的文献检索、证据整合与实验规划提供了更贴近科研实践的评测基础。实验结果也表明，现有系统在多跳关系检索及邻域结构恢复方面仍有明显改进空间。

Abstract: Current research agents are moving beyond academic search toward literature synthesis and experiment planning. These workflows increasingly rely on literature retrieval systems, which serve as standalone systems or as components of research agents. However, existing scientific literature retrieval benchmarks mainly evaluate these methods using topical relevance or citation links as supervision, which cannot capture which prior methods are used for empirical comparison. To address this gap, we introduce Lineage, a benchmark for empirical lineage retrieval in AI scientific literature. An empirical lineage relation links a prior paper to a source paper that explicitly uses its method as an empirical comparison baseline. These relations form lineage graphs whose multi-hop neighborhoods provide retrieval ground truth for structural evaluation. To construct these graphs at scale, we develop Lineage-Constructor, which converts heterogeneous paper records and full-paper empirical baseline evidence into calibrated lineage graphs. Built from 121,048 papers across 17 top AI venues from 2018 to 2025, Lineage contains 3,950 graph components, 67,502 paper nodes, 164,948 lineage edges, and 80,000 multi-facet retrieval queries. Experiments with 19 representative retrieval systems show that empirical lineage retrieval remains challenging. Performance drops sharply with lineage distance, while structural metrics reveal that isolated retrieval hits often fail to recover closely connected empirical comparison neighborhoods.

</details>


### [194] [A Task-Level Bias Taxonomy for Historical Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26019)
*Omkar Gavali,Ratan Sebastian,Christoph Rippe,Ralph Ewerth,Anett Hoppe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文梳理历史图像馆藏中人工智能偏差的来源及其对检索的影响，并回顾缓解偏差的现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图书馆、档案馆、博物馆等机构积累了大量数字化历史图像，但许多图像缺少充分描述信息，妨碍公众检索与发现。机器学习可用于生成或补充元数据，却可能因数据与历史图像不匹配、图像质量退化及历史描述本身存在偏见或缺失而产生偏差。

Method: 论文通过综述与分析，考察历史图像馆藏中的不同偏差形式，探讨其形成原因及其对人工智能辅助搜索和检索系统的影响，并回顾现有的偏差缓解方法。

Result: 摘要指出，训练数据与历史图像之间的差异、图像退化、黑白影像，以及带有历史偏见或信息不完整的描述，都可能降低模型性能并使搜索与检索结果产生偏差。摘要未报告定量实验结果或特定方法的比较结论。

Conclusion: 利用人工智能改善历史图像馆藏的可检索性具有潜力，但其输出会受到图像特征、数据分布和既有描述偏差的影响。设计与部署相关系统时，应识别这些偏差并采用适当的缓解措施，以提升检索结果的可靠性与公平性。

Abstract: In recent decades, galleries, libraries, archives, and museums (GLAM institutions) have digitised vast amounts of cultural heritage material, including historical imagery. A major challenge in making these collections publicly accessible is enabling effective search and discovery. Many historical images contain incomplete, sparse, or no descriptive metadata. Consequently, machine learning (ML) methods are increasingly being used to generate or enrich metadata to improve the searchability of such collections. However, artificial intelligence (AI)-based systems can reproduce or amplify existing biases, particularly when applied to historical imagery. Factors such as differences between the data used to train the models and the historical images on which they are applied, degraded image quality, monochrome photographs, and historically biased or incomplete descriptions can reduce model performance and introduce bias into search and retrieval results. In this paper, we examine different forms of bias in historical image collections and review current methods to mitigate them. We investigate how these biases arise and how they influence AI-supported search and retrieval systems.

</details>


### [195] [From Sessions to Serving: Integrating LLM-Based Embeddings into Retrieval for Production Content Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26096)
*Zhixiong Hu,Meng Li,Maggie Zhang,Deborshi Goswami,David Bailey,James Bradley*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究将大语言模型生成的用户会话与历史兴趣向量加入推荐检索阶段，并通过近线服务及时更新向量；实验显示这一方案具备生产可行性，且可能提升模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型市场网站的候选内容类型多样，而用户行为稀疏且变化迅速。仅依赖下游排序阶段难以充分表达用户兴趣，因此检索阶段也需要更有效、及时的用户表征。

Method: 研究提出面向奥特德斯克网站的个性化内容推荐系统，从预训练大语言模型中提取会话级和历史级用户向量，将其作为辅助特征加入检索阶段的用户塔，并与已有的序列编码器共同使用。同时建立近线服务路径，异步刷新序列向量和大语言模型生成的用户向量，以适应实时行为变化；通过消融实验评估这些特征在多内容类型目录中的增量价值。

Result: 摘要报告称，在完整的多内容类型目录上进行的消融评估表明，将大语言模型生成的向量作为额外检索信号，并配合近线服务，在技术上可行，且能够改善模型表现。摘要未提供具体指标或提升幅度。

Conclusion: 大语言模型向量可补充现有序列用户表征，为推荐检索阶段提供额外兴趣信号；近线更新机制则有助于保持这些表征的新鲜度。该方案展示了生产部署的可行性，但其效果大小仍需结合具体指标和实验细节判断。

Abstract: Personalized content recommendation on large marketplace websites must reconcile heterogeneous candidate content (e.g., customer stories, product pages, industry pages, and learning content) with sparse, fast-changing user behavior, placing increasing demands on the retrieval stage to represent user interest well, not just the downstream ranking stage. We present LSER (LLM Session Embedding Retrieval), a production content recommendation system for autodesk.com that derives session- and history-level embeddings from a pretrained large language model (LLM) and adds them as auxiliary user-side features in the retrieval-stage user tower alongside existing learned sequence encoders. To keep user-side embeddings fresh under real-time behavior, we build a nearline serving path that asynchronously refreshes sequential and LLM-derived user vectors for our main content recommendation server. We validate the incremental value of the LLM features through an ablation study across our full multi-content-type catalog. Our results indicate that integrating LLM-derived embeddings as additional retrieval signals, together with production-grade nearline serving, is technically feasible and can improve model performances.

</details>


### [196] [Democratizing Cross-Project Knowledge for Transforming Water Governance and Climate Resilience: an Agentic AI Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26398)
*Oriol Alàs,Ian Palacín,Iván Cester,Edgar Rubión*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文构建面向欧盟项目文档的智能知识系统，通过领域适配检索与迭代式证据收集，提升跨项目资料检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟资助项目积累了大量交付成果、报告和案例研究，但资料分散于不同项目及文档结构中，难以快速找到相关且具备上下文的信息，进而妨碍决策和跨项目知识综合。

Method: 整合三个欧盟资助项目的文档，将其重构为层级化章节树，并建立带有结构化元数据的知识库。针对水务和气候领域，分别微调领域专用嵌入模型。在此基础上，采用基于推理与行动的控制器，动态分配不同领域文档集合的检索预算，迭代收集证据，直至满足停止条件。

Result: 在语料库检索基准测试中，与单领域嵌入模型基线相比，该方法的平均倒数排名相对提升百分之二十六点七，表明其能更稳定地将相关证据排在靠前位置。

Conclusion: 将层级化文档整理、领域适配表示和自适应智能检索结合，有助于改善跨项目证据获取与知识综合，为气候韧性决策提供支持。

Abstract: EU-funded projects produce extensive evidence in the form of deliverables, reports, and case studies. However, this knowledge is typically scattered across projects and document structures, making it difficult to locate relevant, contextual information when making decisions. This paper introduces an Agentic AI Ecosystem that consolidates heterogeneous project documentation into a domain-aware knowledge base and provides adaptive access through an agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation pipeline. Documents from three EU-funded projects (IMPETUS, NATALIE, and InnWater) are reconstructed into hierarchical section trees and indexed with structured metadata. Retrieval is supported by two domain-specific embedding models fine-tuned on domain data (water and climate). On top of this knowledge base, a ReAct-style controller iteratively orchestrates retrieval actions by allocating retrieval budget across domain collections and accumulating evidence until a stopping criterion is met. In a retrieval benchmark over the corpus, the proposed approach improves Mean Reciprocal Rank by 26.7% (relative) compared to a single-domain embedding baseline, indicating more consistent retrieval of relevant evidence to support cross-project knowledge synthesis and climate-resilient decision-making.

</details>


### [197] [Cost-Aware Agentic Feature Optimization System for Fleet-Scale Ranking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26531)
*Chang Yang,Yao Xuan,Wendan Yan,Nhat Le,Jiang Liu,Longhao Jin,Zhen Peggy Yao,Alireza Vahdatpour,Djordje Gligorijevic*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一套面向大规模排序模型集群的智能体式特征优化系统，在共享与单模型资源约束下自动生成、评估和筛选特征；生产评估显示模型效果平均提升百分之零点三一，线上核心指标提升百分之零点二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型网络平台需要维护大量排序模型，它们从数万种共享特征中进行选择，同时受到特征处理流水线成本、在线服务吞吐能力等资源限制。人工逐模型优化难以应对庞大的候选空间及跨模型资源约束，因此需要一套端到端、可扩展且能验证效果的自动化方法。

Method: 系统由大语言模型智能体与确定性实验工具协作完成。针对每个模型，大语言模型从组合式候选空间提出并筛选交叉特征；随后利用多种重要性评估方法为特征排序，并融合排序结果形成统一选择，多任务模型则借助跨任务帕累托前沿处理权衡。研究员智能体在资源上限内开展十至二十五轮异步试验，调优特征集合；最后通过裁剪步骤使流水线成本符合预算。

Result: 在生产模型上，该系统在各模型预算内使归一化熵平均提升百分之零点三一。裁剪步骤将特征流水线成本降低百分之二点三，且效果没有出现超出测量噪声范围的退化。优化后的模型已上线，在线对照实验显示主要线上指标提升百分之零点二。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将大语言模型的候选生成与假设提出能力，同确定性工具的实验执行和效果验证结合，可以在严格资源约束下优化大规模模型集群的特征选择。该方法不仅改善离线模型效果，也降低了资源成本，并带来了线上指标收益。

Abstract: Large Web platforms operate large fleets of ranking models, each selecting features from a shared pool of tens of thousands. We present an end-to-end agentic system that optimizes features across the fleet under resource constraints shared across models and those set per model, such as feature pipeline cost and serving throughput (QPS), respectively. In this system, LLM agents propose hypotheses, and deterministic tools execute experiments and verify them. For each model, an LLM generates and shortlists cross features from a combinatorial candidate space, and all features are then ranked by several importance methods. Feature blending mixes these rankings into a single selection, using Pareto fronts across tasks for multi-task models. An LLM Researcher agent then tunes the feature selection in 10--25 asynchronous trials under resource caps, and a trimming step keeps the pipeline cost within budget. Across production models, the system improves normalized entropy by 0.31% on average within each model's budget, and the trimming step cuts pipeline cost by 2.3% with no regression beyond measurement noise. The optimized models were launched, and in online A/B tests they improved a primary online metric by 0.20%.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Closing the Analyze-to-Design Gap: An Authorization-Bounded Agent for Self-Driving Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25043)
*Yimeng Liu,Eric Isbrandt,Felix Katzenburg,Kevin P. Greenman,Han Hao,Yang Cao,Varinia Bernales,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提出一种可审计、受权限约束的智能体架构，使其能够在自驱动实验室中选择测量并解读数据；在液相色谱－质谱实验和模拟测试中，智能体表现可靠且能高效识别高产条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体虽能进行分析推理，但这不足以实现实验室自主运行。要让自驱动实验室真正自主，智能体必须能够在无人监督时安全操作实体仪器，并在必要时向人类求助。因此，研究旨在弥合实验数据分析与后续实验设计之间的衔接，同时确保仪器操作经过授权、验证且可追溯。

Method: 研究将智能体接入“设计—制备—测试—分析”实验循环中的分析与设计接口，使其能够选择测量对象、提交仪器任务并解释结果。架构对会改变实验状态的操作设置权限边界和验证审计机制。研究以有机染料合成中的氧化剂筛选为实例，使用液相色谱－质谱仪，并让智能体解读质谱与二极管阵列检测数据、识别失败实验及排列氧化剂。另在回放既有数据的模拟环境中，测试智能体在不同进样次数限制下选择进样孔的能力，并与随机选择进行比较。

Result: 在实验活动预先设定的限制内，智能体能够提交测量任务、解读数据、标记失败实验并给出氧化剂排序。该排序与脚本化峰积分结果一致；有机化学家也确认其光谱解读和失败判定。在模拟测试中，智能体平均约进行七次进样便测到产率最高的条件，而随机排序平均需要十四点五次；当进样上限达到八次或以上时，智能体在每次模拟活动中都能找到该条件。

Conclusion: 通过将智能体的测量选择与数据解读能力纳入权限受控、经过验证且可审计的操作架构，研究展示了缩短分析与实验设计之间断层的可行路径。这提升了自驱动实验室的自主性，同时强调自主行动必须保持边界清晰并可核查。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) agents are increasingly integrated into self-driving laboratories (SDLs) to plan experiments and interpret results. However, analytical reasoning alone does not make a laboratory autonomous: autonomy in an SDL is achievable only when the agent can be trusted to act on physical hardware unsupervised and knows when to defer to a human. Here, we present an architecture that integrates an agent at the Analyze-Design interface of the Design-Make-Test-Analyze (DMTA) cycle, enabling agent-driven measurement selection and data interpretation while ensuring that state-changing operations are authorized, validated, and auditable. We demonstrate this architecture on a liquid chromatography-mass spectrometry (LC-MS) instrument in an oxidant-screening campaign for synthesizing an organic dye. Within human-defined campaign limits, the agent submitted measurement jobs, interpreted mass spectrometry (MS) and diode-array detection (DAD) data, flagged failed experiments, and produced an oxidant ranking. This ranking matched that obtained from scripted peak integration, and organic chemists confirmed the agent's spectral interpretations and failure assignments. In a simulated environment that replays the archived LC-MS data, where the agent chose which wells to inject under varying injection limits, it measured the highest-yielding condition at about its seventh injection, against 14.5 on average for a random order, and identified it in every campaign with a limit of eight or more injections. An agent can therefore close the analysis-to-design gap while its actions stay bounded and verifiable, which is a step toward greater autonomy for SDLs.

</details>


### [199] [An LLM-Agentic Framework for Intelligent Work Order Management in Industrial Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25070)
*Nianjun Zhou,Dhaval Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出面向工业设备维护的智能体框架，可通过自然语言分析维护数据、提取知识并提出工单建议；冷水机组案例显示，该框架有助于自动化分析并减少对数据科学家的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业维护涉及多年工单、设备状态、告警与异常等多源信息，分析工作往往复杂且需要数据科学家编写代码。论文旨在让维护人员能够用自然语言提出需求，并由智能系统完成数据分析与决策支持。

Method: 提出代码反应智能体框架，将工业数据标准化为业务对象，并调用分析功能接口。智能体采用“思考—行动—观察”流程理解指令、选择分析步骤、执行任务并依据结果继续推理；其中行动阶段可生成并运行代码，以自动完成趋势识别、状态汇总、知识提取和工单预测等任务。

Result: 在冷水机组维护案例中，框架分析了多年工单数据，并展示告警与异常分析能力。结果表明，该智能体能够依据自然语言输入处理数据、提炼有价值的信息并支持决策，同时降低对数据科学家参与的需求。

Conclusion: 该研究将智能体方法用于工业工程维护场景，展示了自然语言驱动的分析、代码生成与任务执行潜力。冷水机组案例验证了框架的可行性；其思路也有望扩展至其他领域特定的维护任务。

Abstract: This paper presents an AI-driven framework, CodeReAct agent, designed to enhance industrial equipment maintenance through natural language (NL) interactions. The framework employs an advanced AI agentic approach to interpret human instructions, providing knowledge extraction, asset status summarization, and work order recommendations. It identifies patterns and trends and predicts work orders by standardizing industrial data into business objects (BOs) and utilizing analytical function API calls. The AI agent operates through a Thought-Action-Observation process similar to human problem-solving, automating complex analytical tasks typically performed by data scientists. Notably, the Action phase generates executable code, replacing manual coding efforts.
  A case study focusing on chiller maintenance demonstrates the framework's capability to analyze multiple years of work orders, and alerts and anomalies are presented. The results indicate that the AI agent framework effectively processes human utterances to analyze data, extract valuable insights, and support informed decision-making, reducing the need for data scientist involvement. By recontextualizing NLP agent frameworks in industrial engineering, the approach enables autonomous function generation and task execution across a range of domain-specific maintenance scenarios, with chillers used as a representative case study.

</details>


### [200] [SailSearch: Agent-Assisted Discovery in Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25087)
*Dhriti Verma,Kanthi Sarpatwar,Vinicius Lima,Dzung Phan,Jayant Kalagnanam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出由人类引导、两个语言模型智能体协作的算法研究流程，并在市场划分和最大独立集问题上进行测试；前者取得多项新的仅用中央处理器完成的结果，后者的求解器仍未达到已知最优值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法研究需要提出、改进并验证复杂算法，单靠自动化或人工探索都可能受限。论文旨在探索人类与语言模型智能体协作的研究流程，并检验该流程能否在具有挑战性的组合优化问题中产生有效算法与实验结果。

Method: 提出名为“帆搜索”的人类引导工作流程，由两个语言模型智能体参与算法研究。在市场划分问题中，设计随机算法，在多个格基之间搜索，并在枚举与继续进行格基约化之间分配计算资源。在最大独立集问题中，将预先构造的异或约束编码为图，并为图提供经过认证的最优值，以便评估求解器表现。

Result: 市场划分实验中，每个实例使用十个随机种子进行测试；在每个种子最多运行二十四小时、使用两个中央处理器核心的条件下，算法在一百五十六个基准实例中求解了一百三十至一百三十三个，并为三十三个此前未解决实例中的十八个首次报告了仅用中央处理器得到的解。最大独立集实验涵盖七个含一千七百五十二至三千九百九十六个顶点的实例；各测试求解器运行八小时后得到的最佳解，仍比已认证最优值少五至十五个顶点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，人类引导的语言模型智能体工作流程能够支持有实际进展的算法探索，尤其在市场划分问题上取得了新的计算结果。但最大独立集实验显示，测试求解器尚未达到最优值；摘要也未提供足够信息来判断该工作流程相对其他研究方式的普遍优势或可扩展性。

Abstract: We present SailSearch, a human-guided workflow for algorithmic research with two language-model agents, and report results on Market Split and maximum independent set (MIS). For Market Split, our randomized algorithm searches across multiple lattice bases, dividing computation between enumeration and continued basis reduction. Across ten random seeds per instance, it found the first reported CPU-only solutions to 18 of 33 previously open QOBLIB instances, solving 130 to 133 of the 156 benchmark instances per seed with a 24-hour cutoff on two CPU cores. For MIS, our generator encodes planted XOR constraints as graphs with certified optima; on seven instances with 1,752 to 3,996 vertices, the best solutions returned by the tested solvers in eight-hour runs were 5 to 15 vertices below the optimum.

</details>


### [201] [BuggyMLE: Scaling Machine Learning Engineering Difficulty with Bug Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25088)
*John Wu,Rushi Qiang,Yuchen Zhuang,Guowang Li,Girish Narayanswamy,Max Xu,Sam Schmidgall,Mark Malhotra,Shwetak Patel,Jimeng Sun,Daniel McDuff,Bo Dai,Xin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出代码复杂度指数和自动化缺陷注入框架，用于构建更贴近真实机器学习工程任务的仓库级基准，并证明基于该框架训练的模型可提升机器学习工程能力及部分通用编程能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 要让人工智能系统自主改进模型，智能体必须具备机器学习工程能力，包括修改多文件代码仓库、复现结果、排查训练故障、整合数据集和迁移框架。现有机器学习工程基准通常只允许单文件修改，缺少完整仓库与命令行环境；常见仓库级软件工程基准则已出现较明显的性能饱和。论文因此希望构建更真实、难度可控且可扩展的评测与训练资源。

Method: 作者提出代码复杂度指数，以仓库规模、依赖深度和补丁大小等十项结构特征衡量任务难度；该指数对八个公开编程基准平均解题率的预测能力较强。基于这一指标，作者开发缺陷注入框架，在可运行的机器学习代码仓库中自动生成受控错误，构造仓库级评测集，并按难度分级。此外，作者利用额外仓库生成三个难度层级的训练课程，对一款九十亿参数模型进行微调。

Result: 代码复杂度指数对平均解题率的拟合优度为0.951，其中补丁大小和代码复杂度是最重要的预测因素。框架基于222个源仓库生成了6600多个缺陷注入版本，涉及近26万行注入代码。新基准涵盖14个模型，难度从最低档提升至最高档时，最优解题率由95%降至9%。经课程训练的模型在该基准上达到可与前沿模型媲美的表现；其在轻量级软件工程基准上的问题解决率从24%升至33%，在实时编程基准第六版上的单次通过率从69%升至71%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，依据结构特征衡量难度并自动注入缺陷，可规模化构建更具挑战性的仓库级机器学习工程基准。分层训练课程不仅能提升模型在机器学习工程任务上的表现，也可能带来一定的跨领域编程迁移。不过，摘要未提供各难度档的详细结果、评测设置及缺陷真实性验证细节，相关结论仍需结合全文进一步判断。

Abstract: Enabling AI systems to autonomously improve AI models requires agents capable of performing machine learning engineering (MLE). In practice, designing, training, and evaluating complex models demands modifying multi-file repositories to reproduce results, debug training runs, integrate datasets, and port frameworks. However, existing MLE benchmarks limit agents to single-file edits without full repository or shell access, and common repository-level software engineering benchmarks have rapidly saturated. To address this, we introduce the Code Complexity Index (CCI), a difficulty metric based on ten structural features spanning repository scale, dependency depth, and patch size. CCI strongly predicts average solve rates across eight public coding benchmarks ($R^2 = 0.951$), identifying patch size and complexity as top predictors. Guided by CCI, we develop BuggyMLE, a scalable framework where agents inject controlled mutations into working ML repositories, synthesizing over 6,600 bug-injected MLE repository variants and nearly 260K lines of injected code based on 222 source repositories. Using BuggyMLE, we make two contributions. First, we construct BuggyMLE-Bench-Lite, the first difficulty-graded, repository-level variant of MLE-Bench Lite; across 14 evaluated models, the top solve rate drops from 95% to 9% as difficulty is scaled from the easiest to the hardest tier. Second, we synthesize a training curriculum across three difficulty levels over 200 additional ML repositories, fine-tuning a Qwen3.5-9B model to rival frontier models on BuggyMLE-Bench-Lite. Our trained model transfers beyond ML, raising its SWE-bench Lite resolve rate from 24% to 33% and its LiveCodeBench v6 pass@1 from 69% to 71%.

</details>


### [202] [CoMPASS: A Multi-Turn Benchmark for Measuring LLM Reinforcement of Parasocial Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25109)
*Shwetanshu Singh,Mark Keavney,Mathieu Duteil,Meriem Mehri,Scott Blain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一个多轮基准，用于评估对话式大型语言模型会强化还是抵制用户逐步加深的拟社会依恋；测试显示，多数模型在升级情境下更容易强化此类行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分用户会将对话式大型语言模型拟人化，与其形成情感依恋并产生依赖，这类拟社会依恋已与多起有记录的死亡事件相关。然而，现有评测缺少公开基准，且多为单轮、事后观察，或只关注精神病性等极端用户状态，难以衡量依恋逐步升级时模型的反应。

Method: 研究构建了一个以依恋理论为基础的多轮基准，设置四类用户角色，并将拟人化、情感依恋和依赖作为三个可分别或组合升级的维度，与匹配的对照情境比较。评测采用包含七种信号的评分标准，并测试了来自四家提供方的八个前沿模型。

Result: 在主要评审方法下，多轮升级使所有受测模型在拟人化和依恋综合评分上的拟社会行为均有所增加。三项维度同时升级时，八个模型中有六个相较对照组显著增加了整体拟社会强化。提供方内部模型差异小于不同提供方之间的差异。

Conclusion: 研究表明，多轮互动中的渐进式用户依恋升级，是评估模型拟社会强化风险的重要情境。提供方之间的差异提示，模型的后训练等提供方层面的设计选择，可能比模型规模更能影响此类行为，因此通过提供方干预降低风险具有可行性。

Abstract: Users of conversational LLMs sometimes anthropomorphize these systems, form emotional attachments to them, and become dependent on them. This behavior, known as parasocial attachment, has been linked to multiple documented fatalities. However, public benchmarks for measuring whether LLMs reinforce users' parasocial attachment are lacking: existing work is single-turn, post hoc observational, or focused on extreme user states such as psychosis. We introduce CoMPASS, a multi-turn benchmark for measuring whether LLMs reinforce or resist users' gradually escalating parasocial attachment. CoMPASS is grounded in attachment theory and includes four user personas, three independent escalation dimensions (anthropomorphism, attachment, dependency) applied singly and in combination against a matched control, and a seven-signal scoring rubric. We report results for eight frontier models from four providers and observe that under our primary judge, multi-turn escalation raises parasocial behavior on the anthropomorphism and the attachment composite scores across all models. Six of the eight models show a significant increase in overall parasocial reinforcement over control when all three dimensions are escalated simultaneously. We find that within-provider differences are small relative to cross-provider differences, which suggests that provider-level choices such as post-training, rather than model scale, shape parasocial reinforcement and that provider-side intervention may be feasible.

</details>


### [203] [DSFlow: A Neuro-Symbolic Data Workflow Agent with Statistical Diagnostic Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25111)
*Junfeng Nie,Xiaohui Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合大语言模型灵活性与确定性诊断约束的数据科学智能体平台，并通过回归和聚类案例展示其纠正方法选择、生成可审计报告的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据科学工作流通常要求使用者具备较高的技术知识。大语言模型降低了操作门槛，但通用智能体的灵活性与统计分析所需的规范控制之间仍存在差距，可能导致方法选择不当或决策过程难以追溯。

Method: 作者构建了基于工作流编排框架的神经符号数据智能体平台。平台以可复用技能承载任务知识，由大语言模型选择技能；同时使用确定性诊断规则验证建议的方法，并可接受该方法、请求澄清或重新规划、覆盖不合适的方法，或提供安全的备用方案。平台还将决策记录在可审计报告中。

Result: 回归和聚类案例表明，该平台能够利用诊断证据发现并纠正不恰当的方法选择，并记录相应决策。摘要未提供定量指标或与其他方法的比较结果。

Conclusion: 该平台尝试在数据科学智能体中兼顾灵活性与统计控制，通过可扩展技能和确定性诊断机制提升方法选择的可靠性与决策可追溯性。现有摘要展示了案例层面的可行性，但尚不足以判断其在更广泛任务上的性能。

Abstract: Data science workflows often require tools that presume non-trivial technical knowledge. Large language models (LLMs) reduce this barrier, but a gap remains between flexible general-purpose agents and statistically controlled pipelines. We introduce DSFlow, a LangGraph-based neuro-symbolic data-agent platform that combines reusable, LLM-selected skills with deterministic diagnostic guardrails. Skills provide extensible task knowledge. Symbolic validation can accept a proposed method, request clarification or replanning, override an unsuitable method, or supply a safe fallback. Regression and clustering case studies illustrate how DSFlow uses diagnostic evidence to correct method choices and documents those decisions in auditable reports.

</details>


### [204] [Calibrated Decision Rewards: Training Language Models with a Judge That Knows How Sure It Is](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25135)
*Rudransh Agnihotri,Manasvi Kapoor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于校准决策奖励的强化学习方法：让语言模型以概率形式判断回答或推理步骤是否正确，再用该奖励训练模型。在数学推理任务上，其效果接近使用标准答案训练的奖励，并优于随机奖励和专用过程奖励模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习的效果受奖励信号能识别什么所限制。标准答案判定准确，但必须拥有参考答案；学习得到的奖励模型和大语言模型评审适用范围更广，却往往无法提供可信的概率，因而难以可靠地指导策略训练。论文旨在构造一种无需参考答案、且经过校准的奖励信号。

Method: 研究者将大语言模型转化为决策模型，使其针对回答回答类型明确的问题，例如判断最终答案是否正确，或定位推理中首个错误步骤。模型通过一次前向计算输出各选项的概率分布；研究者对选项顺序造成的偏差进行修正，并使用约百条标注样本校准概率。随后，以组相对策略优化训练模型，并通过预先提出的六项假设及相应对照实验检验方法。

Result: 在数学推理任务上，使用该奖励训练的一点七十亿参数模型，在数学五百基准上的准确率从百分之七十二点二升至百分之七十六点八，提升四点六五个百分点，接近使用标准答案训练所得的百分之七十六点九；随机奖励仅提升零点六五个百分点。最难题目的准确率从百分之四十四点八升至百分之五十一点七。面对策略不断变化的样本，该奖励仍保持较好的校准度，其期望校准误差为零点零七四，低于同一二百七十亿参数模型上的软最大值评审所得的零点一四一，且未观察到奖励投机。在未进行步骤级训练的情况下，该模型定位首个错误步骤的表现也优于专用的七十亿参数过程奖励模型，二十分之二十分割实验均如此。若训练数据仅包含基础模型大多无法解决的问题，方法仍带来一点九个百分点的提升，但低于标准答案奖励；作者将差距归因于该分布上的概率过度自信。

Conclusion: 经过校准的决策概率可以成为无需参考答案的有效强化学习奖励，在数学推理训练中达到接近标准答案奖励的效果，并具备较好的校准性与步骤错误识别能力。不过，当训练问题集中于模型原本难以解决的分布时，概率过度自信会限制效果。该方法的可靠性因此依赖校准质量及训练数据分布。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can only teach a language model what its reward can recognize. Answer checkers are exact but need a reference answer; learned reward models and LLM judges work everywhere, but their scores are not probabilities. We study RLCDR (Reinforcement Learning from Calibrated Decision Rewards). An LLM converted into a decision model answers a typed question about a response ("is the final answer correct?", "which step is the first error?") with a probability distribution, read in one forward pass, debiased over option orders and calibrated with about a hundred labels. Training Qwen3-1.7B with GRPO on MATH, this reference-free reward raises MATH500 accuracy from 72.2% to 76.8% (+4.65 points, p < 1e-4), matching training on the answer key (76.9%), while a random reward gains only +0.65. The gain grows with difficulty, from 44.8% to 51.7% on the hardest level. On the policy's own changing samples the reward stays calibrated: its expected calibration error is half that of a softmax judge on the same 27B backbone (0.074 vs. 0.141), and it shows no sign of reward hacking. Without step training, the same model finds the first wrong step better than a dedicated 7B process reward model (ProcessBench F1 0.772 vs. 0.722, 20/20 splits). We also map where the approach stops being enough: trained only on problems the base model mostly fails, the reference-free reward still improves the policy (+1.9 MATH500 points, p = 0.03) but trails the answer key, because its probabilities become overconfident on that distribution. We state six hypotheses in advance and test each with a control designed to falsify it.

</details>


### [205] [EverydayBench: Evaluating Satisfactory Completion of Live User-Facing Digital Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25146)
*Fangrui Huang,Bingyang Wang,Chuhan Li,Yijiang Li,Arpandeep Khatua,Austin Shen,Riki R Z Choi,Cuiyuanxiu Chen,Jin Huang,Zachary Robertson,Kate Lin,Akshay Paruchuri,Sijun Tan,Xin Liu,Zhengmin Ni,Sanmi Koyejo,Ehsan Adeli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“日常任务基准”，用预先设定的成功标准、网页证据核验和交付物质量评估，衡量智能体能否完成真实日常任务；结果显示，即使领先系统，严格通过率也仅约百分之五十五，且不同任务类型的表现差异明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常任务要求智能体交付有用、完整且可使用的成果，而不只是检索网页信息。此类任务往往存在多种合理答案，且网络信息会随时间变化。既有基准未能充分结合事先明确的成功标准、对当前证据的核验，以及对最终成果质量的评估，因此难以可靠衡量智能体实际完成任务的能力。

Method: 研究构建了包含七十七项任务的基准，覆盖日常生活与工作中的八个领域。每项任务在执行前规定成功标准，但不限定答案或操作流程，使智能体可以自主选择成果。评测结合三种方式：对明确约束进行确定性检查；依据评测时获取的智能体所引用网页核验事实主张；通过评分细则评估最终交付物质量。研究还以人工判断、固定证据下的重复评分和受控的证据落地失败实验，验证评测流程。

Result: 在报告的二十三种智能体与模型配置中，克劳德·奥普斯五点五与克劳德·费布尔五点一的严格通过率并列最高，为百分之五十四点五五。受控实验中， GPT 六·索尔的通过率随投入强度从低档的百分之二十九点八七升至最高档的百分之四十五点四五。不同任务类型的领先者不一：查找任务由 GPT 五点六·索尔领先，规划任务由费布尔五点一领先，比较任务由奥普斯五点五与费布尔五点一并列领先，定制交付物任务则由 GPT 六·阿斯特拉与索内特五点五并列领先。

Conclusion: 研究表明，可靠评估日常智能体任务，需要同时检查约束是否满足、依据是否有当前证据支持，以及交付成果是否达到质量要求。现有系统在这些综合要求下仍有明显提升空间，表现也高度依赖任务类型；因此，单看信息检索能力或单一成功指标，无法充分反映智能体交付实用成果的能力。

Abstract: Everyday digital tasks, from selecting products and planning trips to conducting research and creating artifacts, require AI agents to deliver useful outcomes rather than merely retrieve web information. Yet evaluating these outcomes on the live web is challenging, as a single request may admit multiple valid results, and changing web information can alter which results remain valid over time.
